At this current pace, we’re aiming to have a record-breaking month.\u003cbr\u003e\u003cimg class=\"fr-fic fr-dii\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/026/362/26362-1601948770-8103e955ba84f0f92e96a6adcb6fdd417a240aca.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib fr-fil\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eStay tuned. unless of course, you judge a book by the cover... in which case, try not to be so judgy 😎), then you may recall one of the proprietary technologies that we plan to roll out later this year, IQ2 (Intelligent Qualifier).\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs you may also know, part of our long term vision is to become the largest online marketplace for multifamily, commercial real estate, and business finance in America... and we believe IQ2 is an important part of making our vision a reality. So what’s the main reason for this update?\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBehind the scenes, our superhero data, design, and engineering teams (shoutout to the geniuses) have been heavily focused on building the infrastructure and foundation required to launch the first version of IQ2.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/024/482/24482-1598557581-52cf6c04baaa3a5a903a55cb7f1f20815b3af41d.png\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib fr-fil\" style=\"width: 268px;\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe have officially started testing (and closely monitoring) the first variant of our new lead collection form, which will serve as one of the first touchpoints into the IQ2 platform.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe’re really excited about what IQ2 can bring to the future of commercial real estate finance. As we meticulously measure every step and touchpoint, to ensure optimization for user experience and conversions, reducing frictions in commercial property finance remains at the forefront of our ambitions. With that said, if you’ve already invested please \u003cstrong\u003ere-confirm your investment through Republic\u003c/strong\u003e and if you think it wise, \u003cstrong\u003eincrease your I'm so glad you asked! It's a place where folks from any industry (but particularly residential mortgage lenders, commercial property brokers, real estate finance bloggers, etc.) can generate a unique referral link or manually submit prospects, and then track their entire client life-cycle and deal pipeline natively through our portal... And ultimately watch their fee income grow. It is no small feat to produce something proprietary and so deeply functional as quickly as we did... But that's what we do!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFor a private invite to participate in the beta launch of the platform, shoot us a message and we’ll get you the details.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe'll be focusing on scaling our referral portal, gamifying it and creating network effect opportunities in the comings months. experience, engagement, lead generation, conversions and more. Two of our latest updates involved improving website speed and implementing Google’s new FAQ schema across our website network. Below are some high-value improvements we’ve made recently.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e1) Speed Improvements\u0026nbsp;\u003cstrong\u003e🚀\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWebsite speed plays a big role in ranking on search engines as well as the overall user experience (UX). With this in mind, speed is something we are always working to improve upon. For reference, one of our websites, CMBS.loans, which was already high-performing on Google Search, had an opportunity for additional improvements. So although we were doing an excellent job delivering relevant and valuable content on a responsive platform, there was meaningful room for us to widen our moat.\u003cbr\u003eHere are the results after implementing our updates...\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://lh5.googleusercontent.com/Ca-q4fXxJNNVTZAV_EkGkkAIGZDIMD2ra_GdpK1Cg6m__gWf1ajsndr6yI1W_NmoTvlZlpHFA5qsaT9Luva4hOAHXSHuO37Fj40LzB4K69w5gyHOh0ECE2PuquCza3O0Zghxh217\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dii\" style=\"width: 514px; height: 310.542px;\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFormerly the average mobile user waited on average \u003cstrong\u003e~6 seconds\u003c/strong\u003e to see the first part of the website content, and \u003cstrong\u003e10 seconds\u003c/strong\u003e to visually get the complete website. And as you can see, after the updates users are getting the website loaded in \u003cstrong\u003e~3 seconds\u003c/strong\u003e on a slow 3g network!\u003cbr\u003eDesktop users are getting a full experience in just one, single second 😅\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e2) FAQ Schema 📝\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs noted above, the second thing that we decided to test was to implement Google’s FAQ schema at scale across our sites. Why would we do this? Simple. To get more search engine “real estate” and outperform competitors, by following Google’s guidelines while delivering more value to folks, more quickly, through SERPS.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eHere’s an example:\u003cbr\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://lh5.googleusercontent.com/g-e8qxr1yPQlGIzDw3iMBnmJRiJkKa3xmhbTtzIiAYDBy47dLjI-fOh5YEgVx2O-jiT4vKJzfmq8YQXH9R1cvXxQopzLU2b-xTosFdHpvs-V0_Dz8pP4GETgRWW6TznJ5BRK8PN_\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dii\" style=\"width: 502px; height: 240.96px;\"\u003e\u003cbr\u003eAs a part of the experiment, we’ve created more than 300 instances of FAQ Schemas within our website network.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e✅\u003c/strong\u003eThe Results\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe began the process of speed updates and FAQ schema implementation at the end of March on our playground site, \u003ca href=\"https://cmbs.loans/?utm_source=republic\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ecmbs.loans\u003c/a\u003e and are continuing to roll them out at scale.. According to our Google Analytics, since then we’ve doubled our weekly organic users. Not too shabby!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/021/104/21104-1591466653-00fa95696b61fb7ebb164aa99f364dc46bbe5a27.png\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib fr-fil\" style=\"width: 590px;\"\u003e\u003cbr\u003eFrom a speed perspective, the average page load time before implementing the speed updates was 10 seconds or more. Now it’s as low as \u003cstrong\u003e1.4 seconds\u003c/strong\u003e. That’s very fast! 🚀\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/021/106/21106-1591466851-e7c81855f0879fad41d25e9daf41d9d36e4572e7.png\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib fr-fil\" style=\"width: 592px;\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAccording to Ahrefs, a popular tool for monitoring search engine optimization (SEO), in the month of May we’ve almost doubled our presence in Google Search results, for our CMBS.Loans site!
